947  void tickLand()
948  {
949    if (pilotTookOver("LAND")) return;
950    if (vehicle_state_.mode != "LAND") { trySetMode("LAND"); return; }
951    if (!vehicle_state_.armed) {
952      RCLCPP_INFO(get_logger(), "Landed and disarmed. Mission complete.");
953      transition(State::DONE);
954    } else logWaiting("touchdown / disarm");
955  }

957  void tickAborting()
958  {
959    if (pilotTookOver(p_.abort_mode)) return;
960    if (vehicle_state_.mode == p_.abort_mode) {
961      RCLCPP_WARN(get_logger(), "Vehicle is in %s. Mission node is now passive.", p_.abort_mode.c_str());
962      transition(State::ABORTED);
963      return;
964    }

L1105–1117

main(): single-threaded on purpose

rclcpp::spin uses one thread: callbacks and the timer never run at the same time, so no mutex is needed. A1.2 chose the multi-threaded pattern instead.

23Outdoor mission maths (outdoor_math.h, A1.3)

src/lrs_psenak_valko/src/outdoor_math.h (branch A1_3) · 236 lines · 17 explained blocks

All A1.3 formulas in one header without ROS: angle conventions and wrapping, GPS ↔ metres, the figure-8 and its speed, the soft start/stop, the square and the geofence radius. Because it has no ROS dependency it is unit-tested with plain g++ (next chapter).

1// outdoor_math.h -- A1.3 outdoor mission: all the maths, no ROS dependency.
2//
3// Kept separate from the node so it can be unit-tested with plain g++
4// (see outdoor_math_test.cpp) and so every formula has ONE place to defend.
5//
6// ANGLE CONVENTIONS used throughout this file (state them in the documentation!):
7//   * "ENU yaw"  : radians (or deg where the name says Deg), 0 = East, +CCW.
8//                  This is what /mavros/local_position/pose and the setpoints use.
9//   * "heading"  : degrees, 0 = North, +CLOCKWISE (compass_hdg).
10//   * relation   : yaw_ENU_deg = 90 - heading_deg, wrapped into (-180, 180].
11//
12// FRAME: local ENU, x = East, y = North, z = Up.
13#pragma once
14
15#include <algorithm>
16#include <array>
17#include <cmath>
18#include <limits>
19#include <vector>

L1–20

Conventions in the header

ENU yaw: 0 = East, counter-clockwise positive (pose and setpoints). Compass heading: 0 = North, clockwise (compass_hdg). Relation: yaw_ENU = 90° − heading, wrapped to (−180°, 180°]. Frame: x East, y North, z up.

21namespace outdoor
22{
23
24constexpr double kPi = 3.14159265358979323846;
25constexpr double kDeg2Rad = kPi / 180.0;
26constexpr double kRad2Deg = 180.0 / kPi;
27
28struct Vec2 { double x = 0.0, y = 0.0; };
29struct Vec3 { double x = 0.0, y = 0.0, z = 0.0; };
30
31inline double dist2(const Vec2& a, const Vec2& b) { return std::hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y); }
32inline double dist3(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b)
33{
34  return std::sqrt((a.x - b.x) * (a.x - b.x) + (a.y - b.y) * (a.y - b.y) + (a.z - b.z) * (a.z - b.z));
35}
36

38// Angles
39// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

L21–40

Constants and vectors

π, degree/radian factors, 2D/3D points and their distances.

41/// Wrap into (-pi, pi].
42inline double wrapPi(double a)
43{
44  double r = std::remainder(a, 2.0 * kPi);  // [-pi, pi]
45  if (r <= -kPi) r += 2.0 * kPi;
46  return r;
47}
48
49/// Wrap into (-180, 180].
50inline double wrapDeg180(double a)
51{
52  double r = std::remainder(a, 360.0);
53  if (r <= -180.0) r += 360.0;
54  return r;
55}
56
57/// Wrap into [0, 360).
58inline double wrapDeg360(double a)
59{
60  double r = std::fmod(a, 360.0);
61  if (r < 0.0) r += 360.0;
62  return r;
63}
64
L41–64

Wrapping

remainder gives [−π, π]; the −π end is moved to +π so the range is half-open and every angle has exactly one value. Degree versions to (−180, 180] and [0, 360).

65/// compass heading (0 = N, clockwise, deg) -> ENU yaw (0 = E, CCW, deg) in (-180, 180].
66inline double headingDegToEnuYawDeg(double heading_deg) { return wrapDeg180(90.0 - heading_deg); }
67
68/// ENU yaw (deg) -> compass heading in [0, 360).
69inline double enuYawDegToHeadingDeg(double yaw_deg) { return wrapDeg360(90.0 - yaw_deg); }
70
L65–70

Compass ↔ ENU yaw

The two one-line conversions. North (0°) → yaw 90°, East (90°) → 0°, and back.

71/// Return the angle equivalent to `angle` (mod 2*pi) that lies closest to `reference`.
72/// Applying this to every new yaw sample keeps the sequence continuous (no +-2*pi jumps).
73inline double unwrapNear(double reference, double angle)
74{
75  return reference + std::remainder(angle - reference, 2.0 * kPi);
76}
77
L71–77

unwrapNear()

Same as A1.2: keeps a sequence of yaw commands continuous (no ±2π jump).

78/// Rotate `current` towards `target` by at most `max_step` radians (shortest way round).
79/// `current` stays "unwrapped" (continuous), so repeated calls never spin the long way.
80inline double slewAngle(double current, double target, double max_step)
81{
82  const double d = std::remainder(target - current, 2.0 * kPi);
83  return current + std::clamp(d, -max_step, max_step);
84}
85
L78–85

slewAngle()

Shortest signed difference, clamped to ±max_step, added to the (unwrapped) current angle: a rate-limited turn that never goes the long way.

86/// ENU yaw of the direction from `from` to `to` (0 = East, CCW).
87inline double bearingYaw(const Vec2& from, const Vec2& to)
88{
89  return std::atan2(to.y - from.y, to.x - from.x);
90}
91
92// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
L86–92

bearingYaw()

atan2(Δy, Δx): the ENU direction from one point to another (used for the POI and transit legs).

93// Geodetic <-> local ENU  (local tangent plane, WGS-84 radii at the origin)
94//
95//   east  = dlon * (N + h0) * cos(lat0)
96//   north = dlat * (M + h0)
97//
98// N = prime-vertical radius, M = meridian radius, both evaluated ONCE at the
99// origin.  This is a flat-Earth approximation: the error grows ~ d^2 / (2R), i.e.
100// well below 1 mm at 50 m (measured against GeographicLib in the documentation).
101// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
L93–101

Local tangent plane: the idea

Near the origin the Earth is treated as flat: east = Δlon · (N + h)·cos(lat0), north = Δlat · (M + h), with the WGS-84 radii N (prime vertical) and M (meridian) evaluated once at the origin. The error grows like d²/(2R): far below a millimetre over this mission.

102struct Geodetic { double lat_deg = 0.0, lon_deg = 0.0, alt_m = 0.0; };
103struct Enu { double e = 0.0, n = 0.0, u = 0.0; };
104
105class LocalTangentPlane
106{
107public:
108  LocalTangentPlane() = default;
109  LocalTangentPlane(double lat0_deg, double lon0_deg, double alt0_m)
110  : origin_{lat0_deg, lon0_deg, alt0_m}
111  {
112    constexpr double a = 6378137.0;               // WGS-84 semi-major axis [m]
113    constexpr double f = 1.0 / 298.257223563;     // WGS-84 flattening
114    constexpr double e2 = f * (2.0 - f);          // eccentricity squared
115    const double phi = lat0_deg * kDeg2Rad;
116    const double s = std::sin(phi);
117    const double w = 1.0 - e2 * s * s;
118    const double N = a / std::sqrt(w);
119    const double M = a * (1.0 - e2) / (w * std::sqrt(w));
120    m_per_rad_lat_ = M + alt0_m;
121    m_per_rad_lon_ = (N + alt0_m) * std::cos(phi);
122  }
123
L102–123

LocalTangentPlane: constructor

WGS-84 constants (a = 6 378 137 m, f = 1/298.257…), e² = f(2 − f), then N = a/√(1 − e² sin² φ) and M = a(1 − e²)/(1 − e² sin² φ)^{3/2}. Stored as metres per radian of latitude and of longitude.

124  Enu toEnu(const Geodetic& g) const
125  {
126    return {wrapDeg180(g.lon_deg - origin_.lon_deg) * kDeg2Rad * m_per_rad_lon_,
127            (g.lat_deg - origin_.lat_deg) * kDeg2Rad * m_per_rad_lat_,
128            g.alt_m - origin_.alt_m};
129  }
130
131  Geodetic toGeodetic(const Enu& p) const
132  {
133    return {origin_.lat_deg + p.n / m_per_rad_lat_ * kRad2Deg,
134            origin_.lon_deg + p.e / m_per_rad_lon_ * kRad2Deg,
135            origin_.alt_m + p.u};
136  }
137
138  const Geodetic& origin() const { return origin_; }
139
140private:
141  Geodetic origin_;
142  double m_per_rad_lat_ = 1.0, m_per_rad_lon_ = 1.0;
143};
144
145// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
L124–145

toEnu() / toGeodetic()

Degrees difference → radians → metres (longitude difference wrapped, so crossing ±180° works), and the inverse. Altitude is just the difference.

146// Figure-8 (lemniscate of Gerono), centred on the POI
147//
148//   x(th) = A sin(th)            dx/dth = A cos(th)
149//   y(th) = A sin(th) cos(th)    dy/dth = A cos(2 th)
150//   yaw   = atan2(dy/dth, dx/dth)      (tangent; independent of the time scaling)
151//
152// Speed = A * dth/dt * sqrt(cos^2(th) + cos^2(2 th)); the maximum is A*omega*sqrt(2)
153// at th = 0 and th = pi (the crossing).
154// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
L146–154

Figure-8: formulas

x = A sin θ, y = A sin θ cos θ; derivatives A cos θ and A cos 2θ; tangent yaw = atan2(dy, dx); speed = A·θ̇·√(cos²θ + cos²2θ), maximum A·ω·√2 at the crossing (θ = 0, π).

155struct Figure8Sample { double x, y, dx, dy; };
156
157inline Figure8Sample figure8(double A, double th)
158{
159  return {A * std::sin(th), A * std::sin(th) * std::cos(th), A * std::cos(th), A * std::cos(2.0 * th)};
160}
161
162inline double figure8Yaw(double th) { return std::atan2(std::cos(2.0 * th), std::cos(th)); }
163
164inline double figure8Speed(double A, double omega, double th)
165{
166  return A * omega * std::sqrt(std::cos(th) * std::cos(th) + std::cos(2.0 * th) * std::cos(2.0 * th));
167}
168
169inline double figure8PeakSpeed(double A, double omega) { return A * omega * std::sqrt(2.0); }
170inline double figure8MaxOmega(double A, double v_max) { return v_max / (A * std::sqrt(2.0)); }
171
L155–171

figure8(), yaw, speed

Position and derivative at θ; tangent yaw; speed; peak speed; and the largest ω for a speed limit, v_max / (A√2) = 0.354 rad/s for 1.5 m/s.

172/// Duration of a run from theta = 0 to th_end with a soft start AND a soft stop.  Each ramp
173/// covers only half the phase it would at full rate, so together they cost ramp_s seconds.
174inline double figure8Duration(double omega, double ramp_s, double th_end)
175{
176  return th_end / omega + std::max(ramp_s, 0.0);
177}
178
L172–178

figure8Duration()

Each linear ramp covers only half of what full rate would, so start + stop ramps together cost exactly ramp_s extra seconds.

179/// Phase theta(s) after s seconds.  With ramp_s > 0 the angular rate rises linearly
180/// from 0 to omega during the first ramp_s seconds (theta = omega*s^2 / (2*ramp)),
181/// then stays at omega.  This removes the "lunge": at theta = 0 the curve's speed is
182/// already its maximum, so a hard start would demand ~A*omega*sqrt(2) from standstill.
183/// With a finite th_end the same ramp is mirrored at the end (rate falls to 0 exactly at
184/// th_end), because theta = 2*pi is the crossing point again: a hard stop there would mean
185/// braking from the peak speed in one control tick.
186inline double figure8Phase(double omega, double ramp_s, double s,
187                           double th_end = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity())
188{
189  if (s <= 0.0) return 0.0;
190  if (ramp_s <= 0.0) return std::min(omega * s, th_end);
191  const double T = figure8Duration(omega, ramp_s, th_end);
192  if (s >= T) return th_end;
193  if (s > T - ramp_s) return th_end - 0.5 * omega * (T - s) * (T - s) / ramp_s;
194  if (s < ramp_s) return 0.5 * omega * s * s / ramp_s;
195  return omega * (s - 0.5 * ramp_s);
196}
197
L179–197

figure8Phase(): soft start and soft stop

The rate dθ/dt rises linearly 0 → ω during the first ramp (θ = ω s²/(2·ramp)), stays at ω, and falls back to 0 so that θ reaches exactly 2π at the end. Why: the figure-8 starts and ends at the crossing, where it is fastest (1.27 m/s); a hard start would demand that speed from standstill.

198/// d(theta)/dt at time s -- the analytic derivative of figure8Phase().  Needed for the velocity
199/// feed-forward: velocity = (A cos(th), A cos(2 th)) * figure8PhaseRate().
200inline double figure8PhaseRate(double omega, double ramp_s, double s,
201                               double th_end = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity())
202{
203  if (s <= 0.0) return 0.0;
204  if (ramp_s <= 0.0) return omega * s < th_end ? omega : 0.0;
205  const double T = figure8Duration(omega, ramp_s, th_end);
206  if (s >= T) return 0.0;
207  if (s > T - ramp_s) return omega * (T - s) / ramp_s;
208  if (s < ramp_s) return omega * s / ramp_s;
209  return omega;
210}
211
212// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
L198–212

figure8PhaseRate()

The analytic derivative of the phase, used for the velocity feed-forward (velocity = derivative of position × dθ/dt).

213// Waypoint square around the POI.  Order (seen from above, North up), clockwise:
214//   W1 = NW  ->  W2 = NE  ->  W3 = SE  ->  W4 = SW
215// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
216inline std::array<Vec2, 4> squareCorners(const Vec2& poi, double side)
217{
218  const double h = 0.5 * side;
219  return {{{poi.x - h, poi.y + h}, {poi.x + h, poi.y + h}, {poi.x + h, poi.y - h}, {poi.x - h, poi.y - h}}};
220}
221
L213–221

squareCorners()

W1 = NW, W2 = NE, W3 = SE, W4 = SW around the POI (clockwise seen from above).

222/// Largest horizontal distance from the LAUNCH point (0,0) of anything the mission
223/// visits: the four corners and the whole figure-8.  Used for the geofence check.
224inline double missionMaxRadius(const Vec2& poi, double side, double amplitude)
225{
226  double r = std::hypot(poi.x, poi.y);
227  for (const Vec2& c : squareCorners(poi, side)) r = std::max(r, std::hypot(c.x, c.y));
228  for (int i = 0; i < 720; ++i) {
229    const double th = 2.0 * kPi * i / 720.0;
230    const Figure8Sample s = figure8(amplitude, th);
231    r = std::max(r, std::hypot(poi.x + s.x, poi.y + s.y));
232  }
233  return r;
234}
235
236}  // namespace outdoor
L222–236

missionMaxRadius(): geofence radius

The maximum distance from launch over the POI, the four corners and 720 samples of the figure-8. With the POI at launch: the corners dominate, 2.5·√2 = 3.54 m (limit 4 m).

24Unit test of the maths (outdoor_math_test.cpp, A1.3)

src/lrs_psenak_valko/src/outdoor_math_test.cpp (branch A1_3) · 163 lines · 9 explained blocks

A self-made test program for outdoor_math.h, built with plain g++ (no ROS, no test framework). It prints ALL TESTS PASSED or every failed check with its line.

1// Build & run (no ROS needed):
2//   g++ -std=c++17 -O2 -Wall -Wextra outdoor_math_test.cpp -o outdoor_math_test && ./outdoor_math_test
3#include <cstdio>
4#include <cstdlib>
5
6#include "outdoor_math.h"
7
8using namespace outdoor;
9
10static int g_fail = 0;
11#define CHECK(cond, ...)                                  \
12  do {                                                    \
13    if (!(cond)) {                                        \
14      ++g_fail;                                           \
15      std::printf("FAIL line %d: %s  ", __LINE__, #cond); \
16      std::printf(__VA_ARGS__);                           \
17      std::printf("\n");                                  \
18    }                                                     \
19  } while (0)
20#define NEAR(a, b, tol) CHECK(std::fabs((a) - (b)) <= (tol), "got %.9f expected %.9f", (double)(a), (double)(b))
21
L1–21

How to run it and the CHECK macros

Build command in the first lines. CHECK(cond, ...) counts and prints a failure (with __LINE__ and the condition text #cond); NEAR(a, b, tol) is a CHECK for |a − b| ≤ tol. The do { } while (0) makes a macro behave like one statement.

32    CHECK(y > -180.0 && y <= 180.0, "yaw %.3f out of range", y);

Compass ↔ ENU yaw

The four compass directions map to the expected ENU yaw, the result is always in (−180, 180], and converting back gives the input (round trip every 7.5°).

L36–41

Wrapping edge cases

3π → π, −π → +π, −180° → 180°, −90° → 270°.

42  // ---- geodetic <-> ENU ----------------------------------------------------
43  const LocalTangentPlane ltp(48.1486, 17.1077, 150.0);   // Bratislava-ish, only a test value
44  const Enu east50 = ltp.toEnu({48.1486, 17.1077 + 50.0 / (111320.0 * std::cos(48.1486 * kDeg2Rad)), 150.0});
45  NEAR(east50.e, 50.0, 0.1);                               // ~50 m east (spherical estimate)
46  NEAR(east50.n, 0.0, 1e-6);
47  const Enu p{12.3, -45.6, 4.2};
48  const Enu q = ltp.toEnu(ltp.toGeodetic(p));
49  NEAR(q.e, p.e, 1e-9); NEAR(q.n, p.n, 1e-9); NEAR(q.u, p.u, 1e-9);
50
51  // ---- error of the flat-Earth approximation -------------------------------
52  // Reference = exact conversion geodetic -> ECEF (x, y, z from the Earth's centre) -> ENU
53  // (rotation into east/north/up at the origin), i.e. what GeographicLib computes.
54  // Points on circles of radius 5, 50 and 500 m around the outdoor world's origin.
55  {
56    const double lat0 = 48.15144444, lon0 = 17.07375, h0 = 193.7;
57    const double a = 6378137.0, f = 1.0 / 298.257223563, e2 = f * (2.0 - f);
58    auto ecef = [&](double lat, double lon, double h) {
59      const double p = lat * kDeg2Rad, l = lon * kDeg2Rad;
60      const double N = a / std::sqrt(1.0 - e2 * std::sin(p) * std::sin(p));
61      return std::array<double, 3>{(N + h) * std::cos(p) * std::cos(l), (N + h) * std::cos(p) * std::sin(l),
62                                   (N * (1.0 - e2) + h) * std::sin(p)};
63    };
64    auto exactEnu = [&](const Geodetic& g) {
65      const auto o = ecef(lat0, lon0, h0), c = ecef(g.lat_deg, g.lon_deg, g.alt_m);
66      const double dx = c[0] - o[0], dy = c[1] - o[1], dz = c[2] - o[2];
67      const double p = lat0 * kDeg2Rad, l = lon0 * kDeg2Rad;
68      return Enu{-std::sin(l) * dx + std::cos(l) * dy,
69                 -std::sin(p) * std::cos(l) * dx - std::sin(p) * std::sin(l) * dy + std::cos(p) * dz,
70                 std::cos(p) * std::cos(l) * dx + std::cos(p) * std::sin(l) * dy + std::sin(p) * dz};
71    };
72    const LocalTangentPlane field(lat0, lon0, h0);
73    double err_at[3] = {0.0, 0.0, 0.0};
74    const double radius[3] = {5.0, 50.0, 500.0};
75    for (int k = 0; k < 3; ++k) {
76      for (int i = 0; i < 36; ++i) {                          // every 10 deg around the circle
77        const double az = 2.0 * kPi * i / 36.0;
78        const Geodetic g = field.toGeodetic({radius[k] * std::cos(az), radius[k] * std::sin(az), 0.0});
79        const Enu flat = field.toEnu(g), exact = exactEnu(g);
80        err_at[k] = std::max(err_at[k], std::hypot(flat.e - exact.e, flat.n - exact.n));
81      }
82      std::printf("flat-Earth horizontal error at %5.0f m: %.4f mm\n", radius[k], err_at[k] * 1000.0);
83    }
84    CHECK(err_at[0] < 1e-5, "5 m error %.3g m", err_at[0]);   // our mission: < 0.01 mm
85    CHECK(err_at[1] < 1e-3, "50 m error %.3g m", err_at[1]);  // whole field: < 1 mm
86  }
87

88  // ---- figure-8 ------------------------------------------------------------

90  const Figure8Sample c0 = figure8(A, 0.0);
91  NEAR(c0.x, 0, 1e-12); NEAR(c0.y, 0, 1e-12);              // t = 0 is the centre (POI)

94  // extent 6 x 3 m
95  double xmin = 1e9, xmax = -1e9, ymin = 1e9, ymax = -1e9, vmax = 0;
96  for (int i = 0; i <= 100000; ++i) {
97    const double th = 2 * kPi * i / 100000.0;
98    const Figure8Sample s = figure8(A, th);
99    xmin = std::min(xmin, s.x); xmax = std::max(xmax, s.x);
100    ymin = std::min(ymin, s.y); ymax = std::max(ymax, s.y);
101    vmax = std::max(vmax, figure8Speed(A, 1.0, th));
102  }
103  NEAR(xmax - xmin, 6.0, 1e-6);
104  NEAR(ymax - ymin, 3.0, 1e-6);                            // y in [-A/2, +A/2]
105  NEAR(vmax, A * std::sqrt(2.0), 1e-6);                    // peak speed per omega = A*sqrt(2)
106  NEAR(figure8PeakSpeed(A, 0.35), 1.4849, 1e-3);
107  NEAR(figure8MaxOmega(A, 1.5), 0.3536, 1e-3);

L88–108

Figure-8 geometry and speed

θ = 0 is the centre, tangent 45° there and 135° at θ = π, extent exactly 6 × 3 m, peak speed A√2 per unit ω, and the ω limit 0.354 rad/s for 1.5 m/s.

109  // yaw is continuous after unwrapping: no 2*pi jumps, and no net winding over a lap
110  double yaw = figure8Yaw(0.0), max_step = 0.0, yaw_min = yaw, yaw_max = yaw;
111  const int N = 20000;
112  for (int i = 1; i <= N; ++i) {
113    const double th = 2 * kPi * i / N;
114    const double y_new = unwrapNear(yaw, figure8Yaw(th));
115    max_step = std::max(max_step, std::fabs(y_new - yaw));
116    yaw = y_new; yaw_min = std::min(yaw_min, yaw); yaw_max = std::max(yaw_max, yaw);
117  }
118  CHECK(max_step < 0.01, "max yaw step %.5f rad", max_step);
119  NEAR(yaw, figure8Yaw(0.0), 1e-9);                        // back at the start value: net winding 0
120  std::printf("figure-8 yaw range over a lap: %.1f .. %.1f deg, max step %.4f rad (N=%d)\n",
121              yaw_min * kRad2Deg, yaw_max * kRad2Deg, max_step, N);
122  // The RAW atan2 DOES jump by ~2*pi somewhere -> unwrap is really needed:
123  double raw_max_jump = 0.0, prev = figure8Yaw(0.0);
124  for (int i = 1; i <= N; ++i) {
125    const double y_new = figure8Yaw(2 * kPi * i / N);
126    raw_max_jump = std::max(raw_max_jump, std::fabs(y_new - prev)); prev = y_new;
127  }
128  std::printf("raw atan2 biggest jump: %.3f rad\n", raw_max_jump);

L109–129

Yaw continuity

Over a lap the unwrapped yaw never jumps more than 0.01 rad and ends where it started (no net winding). The raw atan2 does jump by ~2π, which proves unwrapping is needed.

Header and strict mode

Slovak comments; set -euo pipefail as in our scripts.

6ROOT_DIR="$(cd -- "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
7# Nájde koreň repozitára LRS-URK (adresár, v ktorom je scripts/run_gazebo.sh).
8# Poradie: premenná LRS_URK_DIR (aj keď ukazuje omylom na .../scripts), potom okolie tohto
9# adresára, potom bežné miesta v $HOME.
10resolve_lrs_urk() {
11  local c d
12  local candidates=()
13  if [[ -n "${LRS_URK_DIR:-}" ]]; then
14    c="${LRS_URK_DIR%/}"
15    candidates+=("$c" "${c%/scripts}")
16  fi
17  for d in "$ROOT_DIR" "$ROOT_DIR/.." "$ROOT_DIR/../.." "$ROOT_DIR/../../.."; do
18    candidates+=("$d/LRS-URK")
19  done
20  candidates+=("$HOME/Desktop/LRS/LRS-URK" "$HOME/LRS-URK" "$HOME/Desktop/LRS-URK")
21  for c in "${candidates[@]}"; do
22    if [[ -f "$c/scripts/run_gazebo.sh" ]]; then
23      (cd "$c" && pwd)
24      return 0
25    fi
26  done
27  return 1
28}
29
30# Nájde install/setup.bash workspace-u (v tomto adresári alebo o 1-2 úrovne vyššie).
31resolve_install_setup() {
32  local d
33  for d in "$ROOT_DIR" "$ROOT_DIR/.." "$ROOT_DIR/../.."; do
34    if [[ -f "$d/install/setup.bash" ]]; then
35      (cd "$d" && printf '%s/install/setup.bash\n' "$(pwd)")
36      return 0
37    fi
38  done
39  return 1
40}

Usage text

70while (($#)); do
71  case "$1" in
72    --world) (($# >= 2)) || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; WORLD="$2"; shift 2 ;;
73    --square|--square-side|--side) (($# >= 2)) || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; SQUARE_SIDE="$2"; shift 2 ;;
74    --amplitude|--amp) (($# >= 2)) || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; AMPLITUDE="$2"; shift 2 ;;
75    --altitude|--alt) (($# >= 2)) || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; ALTITUDE="$2"; shift 2 ;;
76    --poi-lat) (($# >= 2)) || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; POI_LAT="$2"; shift 2 ;;
77    --poi-lon) (($# >= 2)) || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; POI_LON="$2"; shift 2 ;;
78    --poi-east) (($# >= 2)) || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; POI_EAST="$2"; shift 2 ;;
79    --poi-north) (($# >= 2)) || { usage >&2; exit 2; }; POI_NORTH="$2"; shift 2 ;;
80    --record)
81      RECORD_BAG=1
82      if (($# >= 2)) && [[ "$2" != --* ]]; then
83        BAG_NAME="$2"; shift 2
84      else
85        BAG_NAME="run_$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)"; shift 1
86      fi
87      ;;
88    -h|--help) usage; exit 0 ;;
89    *) EXTRA_NODE_ARGS+=("$1"); shift ;;
90  esac
91done

134open_terminal() {
135  local title="$1"
136  local cmd="$2"
137  if command -v gnome-terminal &>/dev/null; then
138    gnome-terminal --title="$title" -- bash -c "$cmd; echo ''; echo 'Proces skončil. Stlač Enter pre zatvorenie okna...'; read" &
139  elif command -v xterm &>/dev/null; then
140    xterm -title "$title" -e bash -c "$cmd; echo ''; echo 'Proces skončil. Stlač Enter pre zatvorenie okna...'; read" &
141  else
142    echo "Upozornenie: gnome-terminal ani xterm nenájdený, spúšťam na pozadí..."
143    bash -c "$cmd" &
144  fi
145}

147cleanup() {
148  trap - EXIT INT TERM

150  echo "==> Ukončujem procesy simulácie a misie (len tvoje, user=$USER)..."
151  # Zabíjame len procesy tohto používateľa; -f hľadá v celej príkazovej riadke.

Node parameters

Converts options to floating-point text (ROS rejects "5" for a double parameter) and collects the -p arguments.

218# Geofence automaticky prispôsobíme, aby misia neabortla sama. Pri POI zadanom ako lat/lon
219# sa vzdialenosť od štartu zistí až v uzle, preto sem pridáme rezervu podľa --poi-east/north
220# (pri lat/lon nechaj limit explicitne cez -p max_horizontal_from_launch_m:=...).
221if [[ -n "$SQUARE_SIDE$AMPLITUDE$ALTITUDE$POI_EAST$POI_NORTH" ]]; then
222  S_VAL="${SQUARE_SIDE:-5.0}"
223  A_VAL="${AMPLITUDE:-3.0}"
224  Z_VAL="${ALTITUDE:-5.0}"
225  E_VAL="${POI_EAST:-0.0}"
226  N_VAL="${POI_NORTH:-0.0}"
227  LIMITS=$(python3 -c "
228import math, sys
229s, a, z, e, n = (float(v) for v in sys.argv[1:6])
230req_r = math.hypot(e, n) + max(s * math.sqrt(2.0) / 2.0, a)
231max_h = float(max(4.0, math.ceil(req_r + 1.0)))
232max_z = float(max(5.5, math.ceil(z + 1.0)))
233print(f'{max_h:.2f} {max_z:.2f}')
234" "$S_VAL" "$A_VAL" "$Z_VAL" "$E_VAL" "$N_VAL")
235  MAX_H=$(echo "$LIMITS" | cut -d' ' -f1)
236  MAX_Z=$(echo "$LIMITS" | cut -d' ' -f2)
237  NODE_PARAMS+=("-p" "max_horizontal_from_launch_m:=$MAX_H" "-p" "max_altitude_m:=$MAX_Z")
238fi

26Plots from CSV or rosbag (plot_rosbag.py, A1.3)

plot_rosbag.py (branch A1_3) · 564 lines · 12 explained blocks

Reads the node's CSV log or a rosbag and draws the square with the POI, the figure-8, the yaw error and a six-panel overview. Slovak labels.

1#!/usr/bin/env python3
2"""
3plot_rosbag.py
4==============
5Vykreslí prejdenú a požadovanú dráhu z ROS 2 bagu alebo CSV logu:
6  1. Samostatný pôdorys: Štvorec (W1..W4) so smerovaním nosa na POI
7  2. Samostatný pôdorys: Osmička (Lemniskáta) s dotyčnicovým smerovaním nosa
8  3. Odchýlka natočenia nosa (Yaw error [°]) voči POI / dotyčnici
9  4. Priebeh Yaw v čase (požadovaný vs nameraný)
10  5. Výškový profil (Z vs Čas)
11  6. Polohová odchýlka (Tracking Error [m])
12
13Použitie:
14    python3 plot_rosbag.py                      # automaticky nájde outdoor_mission_log.csv
15    python3 plot_rosbag.py cesta/k/bagu         # z ROS 2 bagu
16    python3 plot_rosbag.py --csv moj_log.csv    # z vlastného CSV
17    python3 plot_rosbag.py --no-show            # iba uloží obrázky bez GUI
18"""
19
20import argparse
21import csv
22import math
23import os
24import sys
25
26try:
27    import matplotlib
28    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
29    from matplotlib.gridspec import GridSpec
30except ImportError:
31    print("Chyba: Matplotlib nie je nainštalovaný. Spustite: pip install matplotlib", file=sys.stderr)
32    sys.exit(1)

Docstring and imports

What is drawn and how to call it; matplotlib is required.

35def parse_csv_log(csv_path):
36    """Načíta dáta z CSV logu vygenerovaného outdoor_mission_node."""
37    print(f"--> Načítavam dáta z CSV: {csv_path}")
38    data = {
39        't': [], 'state': [],
40        'cmd_x': [], 'cmd_y': [], 'cmd_z': [], 'cmd_yaw': [],
41        'x': [], 'y': [], 'z': [], 'yaw': [],
42        'yaw_err': [], 'poi_err': [],
43        'track_err': [], 'rel_alt': []
44    }
45    def to_float(val, default=0.0):
46        if val is None or val == '' or str(val).lower() == 'nan':
47            return default
48        try:
49            return float(val)
50        except (ValueError, TypeError):
51            return default
52
53    def to_float_or_nan(val):
54        if val is None or val == '' or str(val).lower() == 'nan':
55            return float('nan')
56        try:
57            return float(val)
58        except (ValueError, TypeError):
59            return float('nan')
60
61    with open(csv_path, 'r', newline='') as f:
62        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
63        for row in reader:
64            try:
65                # Ak je riadok neúplný (napr. prerušený pri Ctrl+C)
66                if not row or row.get('t_s') is None or row.get('x') is None:
67                    continue
68
69                t = to_float(row.get('t_s'))
70                state = str(row.get('state') or '').strip()
71                cx = to_float_or_nan(row.get('cmd_x'))
72                cy = to_float_or_nan(row.get('cmd_y'))
73                cz = to_float_or_nan(row.get('cmd_z'))
74                cyaw = to_float_or_nan(row.get('cmd_yaw_deg'))
75
76                x = to_float(row.get('x'))
77                y = to_float(row.get('y'))
78                z = to_float(row.get('z'))
79                yaw = to_float(row.get('yaw_deg'))
80
81                yerr = to_float_or_nan(row.get('yaw_err_deg'))
82                perr = to_float_or_nan(row.get('poi_bearing_err_deg'))
83
84                err = to_float_or_nan(row.get('track_err_m'))
85                rel = to_float(row.get('rel_alt'), default=z)
86
87                data['t'].append(t)
88                data['state'].append(state)
89                data['cmd_x'].append(cx)
90                data['cmd_y'].append(cy)
91                data['cmd_z'].append(cz)
92                data['cmd_yaw'].append(cyaw)
93                data['x'].append(x)
94                data['y'].append(y)
95                data['z'].append(z)
96                data['yaw'].append(yaw)
97                data['yaw_err'].append(yerr)
98                data['poi_err'].append(perr)
99                data['track_err'].append(err)
100                data['rel_alt'].append(rel)
101            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
102                continue
103    return data
104

parse_csv_log()

106def parse_rosbag(bag_path):
107    """Načíta dáta z ROS 2 bag priečinka pomocou rosbag2_py."""
108    print(f"--> Načítavam dáta z ROS 2 bagu: {bag_path}")
109    try:
110        import rosbag2_py
111        from rclpy.serialization import deserialize_message
112        from rosidl_runtime_py.utilities import get_message
113    except ImportError as e:
114        print(f"Upozornenie: rosbag2_py alebo rclpy nie je dostupné ({e}).", file=sys.stderr)
115        return None
116
117    storage_options = rosbag2_py.StorageOptions(uri=bag_path)
118    converter_options = rosbag2_py.ConverterOptions(
119        input_serialization_format='cdr',
120        output_serialization_format='cdr'
121    )
122
123    try:
124        reader = rosbag2_py.SequentialReader()
125        reader.open(storage_options, converter_options)
126    except Exception as e:
127        print(f"Chyba pri otváraní bagu: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
128        return None
129
130    topic_types = reader.get_all_topics_and_types()
131    type_map = {t.name: t.type for t in topic_types}
132
133    wanted_topics = {
134        '/mavros/local_position/pose',
135        '/mavros/setpoint_raw/local',
136        '/mission/intended_path',
137        '/mission/state'
138    }
139
140    flown = {'t': [], 'x': [], 'y': [], 'z': [], 'yaw': []}
141    target = {'t': [], 'cmd_x': [], 'cmd_y': [], 'cmd_z': [], 'cmd_yaw': []}
142    states = {'t': [], 'state': []}
143
144    t0 = None
145
L106–145

parse_rosbag(): open the bag

rosbag2_py.SequentialReader with CDR serialization, then a map topic → type.

146    while reader.has_next():
147        topic, data_bytes, stamp_ns = reader.read_next()
148        if topic not in wanted_topics:
149            continue
150
151        if t0 is None:
152            t0 = stamp_ns
153        t_sec = (stamp_ns - t0) * 1e-9
154
155        msg_type_name = type_map.get(topic)
156        if not msg_type_name:
157            continue
158
159        msg_cls = get_message(msg_type_name)
160        msg = deserialize_message(data_bytes, msg_cls)
161
162        if topic == '/mavros/local_position/pose':
163            pos = msg.pose.position
164            ori = msg.pose.orientation
165            yaw_rad = math.atan2(2.0 * (ori.w * ori.z + ori.x * ori.y),
166                                 1.0 - 2.0 * (ori.y * ori.y + ori.z * ori.z))
167            flown['t'].append(t_sec)
168            flown['x'].append(pos.x)
169            flown['y'].append(pos.y)
170            flown['z'].append(pos.z)
171            flown['yaw'].append(math.degrees(yaw_rad))
172
173        elif topic == '/mavros/setpoint_raw/local':
174            target['t'].append(t_sec)
175            target['cmd_x'].append(msg.position.x)
176            target['cmd_y'].append(msg.position.y)
177            target['cmd_z'].append(msg.position.z)
178            # uzol posiela rozbalený (spojitý) yaw; na graf ho zalomíme do (-180, 180] ako CSV log
179            target['cmd_yaw'].append((math.degrees(msg.yaw) + 180.0) % 360.0 - 180.0)
180
181        elif topic == '/mission/state':
182            states['t'].append(t_sec)
183            states['state'].append(msg.data)
184
185    if not flown['t']:
186        print("V bagu sa nenašli žiadne dáta pre /mavros/local_position/pose!", file=sys.stderr)
187        return None
188    if not states['t']:
189        print("Upozornenie: bag neobsahuje /mission/state (starší záznam) -- fázy sa nerozlíšia, "
190              "pre rozdelené grafy použi --csv.", file=sys.stderr)
191
L146–191

Read the messages

Deserialize pose (yaw from the quaternion), setpoints (yaw wrapped) and the state topic, with times relative to the first message.

192    # Setpointy a stavy chodia v iných časoch ako póza: ku každej vzorke pózy priradíme posledný
193    # setpoint / stav pred ňou (zero-order hold).  Setpoint starší ako 0.5 s sa nepočíta (vtedy
194    # uzol nestreamuje -- vzlet, pristátie), aby grafy neukazovali falošné chyby.
195    nan = float('nan')
196    data = {k: [] for k in ('t', 'state', 'x', 'y', 'z', 'yaw', 'cmd_x', 'cmd_y', 'cmd_z', 'cmd_yaw',
197                            'yaw_err', 'poi_err', 'track_err', 'rel_alt')}
198    j = k = -1
199    for i, t in enumerate(flown['t']):
200        while j + 1 < len(target['t']) and target['t'][j + 1] <= t:
201            j += 1
202        while k + 1 < len(states['t']) and states['t'][k + 1] <= t:
203            k += 1
204        state = states['state'][k] if k >= 0 else 'UNKNOWN'
205        x, y, z, yaw = flown['x'][i], flown['y'][i], flown['z'][i], flown['yaw'][i]
206        live = j >= 0 and t - target['t'][j] <= 0.5
207        cx = target['cmd_x'][j] if live else nan
208        cy = target['cmd_y'][j] if live else nan
209        cz = target['cmd_z'][j] if live else nan
210        cyaw = target['cmd_yaw'][j] if live else nan
211        # POI = stred misie (0, 0), rovnako ako v ostatných grafoch tohto skriptu
212        poi_err = nan
213        if state == 'SQUARE' and math.hypot(x, y) > 0.5:
214            poi_err = (yaw - math.degrees(math.atan2(-y, -x)) + 180.0) % 360.0 - 180.0
215        data['t'].append(t)
216        data['state'].append(state)
217        data['x'].append(x); data['y'].append(y); data['z'].append(z); data['yaw'].append(yaw)
218        data['cmd_x'].append(cx); data['cmd_y'].append(cy); data['cmd_z'].append(cz); data['cmd_yaw'].append(cyaw)
219        data['yaw_err'].append((yaw - cyaw + 180.0) % 360.0 - 180.0 if live else nan)
220        data['poi_err'].append(poi_err)
221        data['track_err'].append(math.sqrt((x - cx) ** 2 + (y - cy) ** 2 + (z - cz) ** 2) if live else nan)
222        data['rel_alt'].append(z)
223    return data
224
225
L192–225

Align setpoints and states with the pose

For each pose sample, take the last setpoint and state before it (zero-order hold). A setpoint older than 0.5 s is treated as "not streaming" (NaN), so takeoff/landing do not show fake errors. Then the POI and tracking errors.

226def filter_by_states(data, valid_states):
227    """Vráti podsadu dát pre dané letové stavy."""
228    indices = [i for i, s in enumerate(data.get('state', [])) if s in valid_states]
229    sub = {}
230    for k, v in data.items():
231        if isinstance(v, list) and len(v) == len(data['t']):
232            sub[k] = [v[i] for i in indices]
233        else:
234            sub[k] = v
235    return sub
236
237
L226–237

filter_by_states()

Keeps only the samples of chosen phases (e.g. SQUARE).

238def detect_geometry(data, user_square=None, user_amp=None):
239    """Zistí alebo automaticky deteguje dĺžku štvorca a amplitúdu osmičky."""
240    if user_square is not None:
241        square_side = user_square
242    else:
243        sq_x = [abs(cx) for cx, st in zip(data.get('cmd_x', []), data.get('state', []))
244                if st == 'SQUARE' and not math.isnan(cx)]
245        square_side = round(2.0 * max(sq_x), 2) if sq_x else 5.0
246
247    if user_amp is not None:
248        amplitude = user_amp
249    else:
250        f8_x = [abs(cx) for cx, st in zip(data.get('cmd_x', []), data.get('state', []))
251                if st == 'FIGURE8' and not math.isnan(cx)]
252        amplitude = round(max(f8_x), 2) if f8_x else 3.0
253
254    return square_side, amplitude
255

257def plot_single_square(data_sq, square_side=5.0, output_file=None):
258    """Vykreslí samostatný detailný graf štvorca s orientáciou na POI."""
259    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 7))
260    ax.set_title(f'A1.3 — Pôdorys dráhy: Štvorec ({square_side}×{square_side} m) s orientáciou nosa na POI', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
261
262    half = square_side / 2.0
263    w_corners = [(-half, half), (half, half), (half, -half), (-half, -half), (-half, half)]
264    cx = [p[0] for p in w_corners]
265    cy = [p[1] for p in w_corners]
266    ax.plot(cx, cy, 'k--', linewidth=1.5, label=f'Požadovaný štvorec ({square_side}×{square_side} m)')
267
268    # Nameraná dráha štvorca
269    if data_sq.get('x'):
270        ax.plot(data_sq['x'], data_sq['y'], 'b-', linewidth=2.0, label='Prejdená dráha drona')
271
272        # Vektorové šípky natočenia nosa (každých N bodov)
273        step = max(1, len(data_sq['x']) // 25)
274        for i in range(0, len(data_sq['x']), step):
275            px, py = data_sq['x'][i], data_sq['y'][i]
276            yaw_rad = math.radians(data_sq['yaw'][i])
277            u = 0.4 * math.cos(yaw_rad)
278            v = 0.4 * math.sin(yaw_rad)
279            ax.arrow(px, py, u, v, head_width=0.12, head_length=0.15, fc='cyan', ec='darkblue', alpha=0.8)
280
281    # POI
282    ax.scatter([0.0], [0.0], color='red', s=200, marker='*', zorder=6, label='POI (Point of Interest)')
283
284    # Rohy W1..W4
285    labels = [
286        f'W1 (-{half:.1f}, {half:.1f})',
287        f'W2 ({half:.1f}, {half:.1f})',
288        f'W3 ({half:.1f}, -{half:.1f})',
289        f'W4 (-{half:.1f}, -{half:.1f})'
290    ]
291    for i, (wx, wy) in enumerate(w_corners[:4]):
292        ax.scatter([wx], [wy], color='darkorange', s=80, zorder=5)
293        ax.annotate(f" {labels[i]}", (wx, wy), fontsize=9, fontweight='bold')
294
295    ax.set_xlabel('East [m]')
296    ax.set_ylabel('North [m]')
297    ax.axis('equal')
298    ax.grid(True, linestyle=':', alpha=0.6)
299    ax.legend(loc='best', fontsize=9)
300
301    if output_file:
302        plt.savefig(output_file, dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
303        print(f"==> Samostatný graf štvorca uložený do: {output_file}")
304    plt.close(fig)
305

307def plot_single_figure8(data_f8, amplitude=3.0, output_file=None):
308    """Vykreslí samostatný detailný graf osmičky s dotyčnicovým yaw."""
309    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 7))
310    ax.set_title(f'A1.3 — Pôdorys dráhy: Osmička (Figure-8 lemniskáta A={amplitude} m)', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
311
312    # Teoretická ideálna osmička
313    t_vals = [2.0 * math.pi * i / 200.0 for i in range(201)]
314    ideal_x = [amplitude * math.sin(t) for t in t_vals]
315    ideal_y = [amplitude * math.sin(t) * math.cos(t) for t in t_vals]
316    ax.plot(ideal_x, ideal_y, 'k--', linewidth=1.5, label=f'Požadovaná osmička (A={amplitude} m)')
317
318    # Nameraná dráha osmičky
319    if data_f8.get('x'):
320        ax.plot(data_f8['x'], data_f8['y'], 'm-', linewidth=2.0, label='Prejdená dráha drona')
321
322        # Vektorové šípky natočenia nosa pozdĺž dotyčnice
323        step = max(1, len(data_f8['x']) // 25)
324        for i in range(0, len(data_f8['x']), step):
325            px, py = data_f8['x'][i], data_f8['y'][i]
326            yaw_rad = math.radians(data_f8['yaw'][i])
327            u = 0.35 * math.cos(yaw_rad)
328            v = 0.35 * math.sin(yaw_rad)
329            ax.arrow(px, py, u, v, head_width=0.1, head_length=0.12, fc='yellow', ec='purple', alpha=0.8)
330
331    ax.scatter([0.0], [0.0], color='red', s=180, marker='*', zorder=6, label='Stred osmičky (POI)')
332
333    ax.set_xlabel('East [m]')
334    ax.set_ylabel('North [m]')
335    ax.axis('equal')
336    ax.grid(True, linestyle=':', alpha=0.6)
337    ax.legend(loc='best', fontsize=9)
338
339    if output_file:
340        plt.savefig(output_file, dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
341        print(f"==> Samostatný graf osmičky uložený do: {output_file}")
342    plt.close(fig)
343

Figure-8 plot

345def plot_single_yaw_error(data, output_file=None):
346    """Vykreslí samostatný detailný graf odchýlky natočenia nosa drona."""
347    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 5))
348    ax.set_title('A1.3 — Uhlová odchýlka natočenia nosa drona (Yaw Error [°])', fontsize=12, fontweight='bold')
349
350    # Odchýlka voči POI počas štvorca
351    t_poi = []
352    err_poi = []
353    t_f8 = []
354    err_f8 = []
355
356    for i in range(len(data['t'])):
357        st = data['state'][i] if 'state' in data else ''
358        t = data['t'][i]
359
360        if st in ('TO_START', 'SQUARE') and i < len(data.get('poi_err', [])):
361            val = data['poi_err'][i]
362            if not math.isnan(val):
363                t_poi.append(t)
364                err_poi.append(abs(val))
365
366        elif st == 'FIGURE8' and i < len(data.get('yaw_err', [])):
367            val = data['yaw_err'][i]
368            if not math.isnan(val):
369                t_f8.append(t)
370                err_f8.append(abs(val))
371
372    if err_poi:
373        avg_poi = sum(err_poi) / len(err_poi)
374        max_poi = max(err_poi)
375        ax.plot(t_poi, err_poi, 'b-', linewidth=1.8, label=f'Chyba voči POI počas štvorca (Priemer: {avg_poi:.1f}°, Max: {max_poi:.1f}°)')
376
377    if err_f8:
378        avg_f8 = sum(err_f8) / len(err_f8)
379        max_f8 = max(err_f8)
380        ax.plot(t_f8, err_f8, 'm-', linewidth=1.8, label=f'Chyba voči dotyčnici počas osmičky (Priemer: {avg_f8:.1f}°, Max: {max_f8:.1f}°)')
381
382    ax.axhline(10.0, color='orange', linestyle='--', alpha=0.7, label='Referenčná tolerancia (10°)')
383    ax.set_xlabel('Čas [s]')
384    ax.set_ylabel('|Chyba Yaw| [°]')
385    ax.set_ylim(bottom=0.0)
386    ax.grid(True, linestyle=':', alpha=0.6)
387    ax.legend(loc='best', fontsize=9)
388
389    if output_file:
390        plt.savefig(output_file, dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')
391        print(f"==> Samostatný graf chyby yaw uložený do: {output_file}")
392    plt.close(fig)
393
394
L345–394

|nose − bearing to POI| during the square and |nose − tangent| during the figure-8, with mean and maximum in the legend and a 10° reference line.

395def plot_comprehensive(data, square_side=5.0, amplitude=3.0, output_file=None, show=True):
396    """Vykreslí komplexný 6-panelový graf misie."""
397    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(16, 12))
398    gs = GridSpec(3, 2, figure=fig, hspace=0.35, wspace=0.25)
399    fig.suptitle('A1.3 Outdoor GPS Misia — Komplexné vyhodnotenie letu', fontsize=16, fontweight='bold')
400
401    has_states = 'state' in data and any(s in ('SQUARE', 'FIGURE8') for s in data['state'])
402
403    # 1. Pôdorys: ŠTVOREC (W1..W4)
404    ax1 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 0])
405    ax1.set_title(f'1. Dráha štvorca ({square_side}×{square_side} m, POI tracking)', fontweight='bold')

407    w_corners = [(-half, half), (half, half), (half, -half), (-half, -half), (-half, half)]
408    ax1.plot([p[0] for p in w_corners], [p[1] for p in w_corners], 'k--', linewidth=1.5, label=f'Požadovaný štvorec ({square_side}×{square_side} m)')
409
410    data_sq = filter_by_states(data, {'TO_START', 'SQUARE'}) if has_states else data
411    if data_sq.get('x'):
412        ax1.plot(data_sq['x'], data_sq['y'], 'b-', linewidth=2.0, label='Nameraná dráha')
413    ax1.scatter([0.0], [0.0], color='red', s=160, marker='*', zorder=6, label='POI')
414    for wx, wy in w_corners[:4]:
415        ax1.scatter([wx], [wy], color='darkorange', s=50, zorder=5)
416    ax1.set_xlabel('East [m]')
417    ax1.set_ylabel('North [m]')
418    ax1.axis('equal')
419    ax1.grid(True, linestyle=':', alpha=0.6)
420    ax1.legend(loc='best', fontsize=8)
421
422    # 2. Pôdorys: OSMIČKA (Lemniskáta)
423    ax2 = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 1])
424    ax2.set_title(f'2. Dráha osmičky (Figure-8 A={amplitude} m)', fontweight='bold')
425    t_vals = [2.0 * math.pi * i / 200.0 for i in range(201)]
426    ax2.plot([amplitude * math.sin(t) for t in t_vals], [amplitude * math.sin(t) * math.cos(t) for t in t_vals],
427             'k--', linewidth=1.5, label=f'Požadovaná osmička (A={amplitude} m)')
428
429    data_f8 = filter_by_states(data, {'FIGURE8'}) if has_states else data
430    if data_f8.get('x'):
431        ax2.plot(data_f8['x'], data_f8['y'], 'm-', linewidth=2.0, label='Nameraná dráha')
432    ax2.scatter([0.0], [0.0], color='red', s=160, marker='*', zorder=6, label='Stred (POI)')
433    ax2.set_xlabel('East [m]')
434    ax2.set_ylabel('North [m]')
435    ax2.axis('equal')
436    ax2.grid(True, linestyle=':', alpha=0.6)
437    ax2.legend(loc='best', fontsize=8)
438
439    # 3. Odchýlka natočenia nosa (YAW ERROR)
440    ax3 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 0])
441    ax3.set_title('3. Uhlová odchýlka natočenia nosa (|Chyba Yaw| [°])', fontweight='bold')
442    t_poi, err_poi, t_f8, err_f8 = [], [], [], []
443    for i in range(len(data['t'])):
444        st = data['state'][i] if has_states else ''
445        t = data['t'][i]
446        if st in ('TO_START', 'SQUARE') and i < len(data.get('poi_err', [])):
447            v = data['poi_err'][i]
448            if not math.isnan(v): t_poi.append(t); err_poi.append(abs(v))
449        elif st == 'FIGURE8' and i < len(data.get('yaw_err', [])):
450            v = data['yaw_err'][i]
451            if not math.isnan(v): t_f8.append(t); err_f8.append(abs(v))
452
453    if err_poi:
454        ax3.plot(t_poi, err_poi, 'b-', linewidth=1.5, label=f'Štvorec -> POI (Ø {sum(err_poi)/len(err_poi):.1f}°)')
455    if err_f8:
456        ax3.plot(t_f8, err_f8, 'm-', linewidth=1.5, label=f'Osmička -> Dotyčnica (Ø {sum(err_f8)/len(err_f8):.1f}°)')
457    ax3.axhline(10.0, color='orange', linestyle='--', alpha=0.6, label='Tolerancia (10°)')
458    ax3.set_xlabel('Čas [s]')
459    ax3.set_ylabel('|Chyba Yaw| [°]')
460    ax3.set_ylim(bottom=0.0)
461    ax3.grid(True, linestyle=':', alpha=0.6)
462    ax3.legend(loc='best', fontsize=8)
463
464    # 4. Priebeh Yaw v čase (Požadovaný vs Nameraný)
465    ax4 = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 1])
466    ax4.set_title('4. Priebeh Yaw v čase (ENU [°])', fontweight='bold')
467    if data.get('cmd_yaw'):
468        ax4.plot(data['t'], data['cmd_yaw'], 'r--', linewidth=1.3, label='Požadovaný yaw')
469    ax4.plot(data['t'], data['yaw'], 'b-', linewidth=1.4, alpha=0.8, label='Nameraný yaw')
470    ax4.set_xlabel('Čas [s]')

505        print("==> Otváram interaktívne okno grafu...")

507    else:
508        plt.close(fig)

511def main():
512    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Vykreslenie prejdenej dráhy z ROS 2 bagu alebo CSV logu.")
513    parser.add_argument("bag_path", nargs="?", default=None, help="Cesta k priečinku ROS 2 bagu.")
514    parser.add_argument("--csv", default=None, help="Cesta k CSV log súboru (napr. outdoor_mission_log.csv).")
515    parser.add_argument("--output", "-o", default="outdoor_mission_plot.png", help="Názov hlavného obrázka.")
516    parser.add_argument("--square", "--square-side", type=float, default=None, help="Dĺžka strany štvorca v metroch (ak nezadané, deteguje sa z dát).")
517    parser.add_argument("--amplitude", "--amp", type=float, default=None, help="Amplitúda/polomer osmičky A v metroch (ak nezadané, deteguje sa z dát).")
518    parser.add_argument("--no-show", action="store_true", help="Nezobrazovať interaktívne grafické okno, iba uložiť obrázky.")
519
520    args = parser.parse_args()
521
522    data = None
523    if args.csv:
524        if not os.path.exists(args.csv):
525            print(f"Chyba: CSV súbor nebol nájdený: {args.csv}", file=sys.stderr)
526            sys.exit(1)
527        data = parse_csv_log(args.csv)
528    elif args.bag_path:
529        if not os.path.exists(args.bag_path):
530            print(f"Chyba: Bag priečinok nebol nájdený: {args.bag_path}", file=sys.stderr)
531            sys.exit(1)
532        data = parse_rosbag(args.bag_path)
533    else:
534        default_csv = "outdoor_mission_log.csv"
535        if os.path.exists(default_csv):
536            print(f"Nebol zadaný argument, našiel som {default_csv}. Použijem ho.")
537            data = parse_csv_log(default_csv)
538        else:
539            parser.print_help()
540            sys.exit(1)
541
542    if not data or not data.get('t'):
543        print("Chyba: Nepodarilo sa načítať žiadne platné dáta na vykreslenie.", file=sys.stderr)
544        sys.exit(1)
545
546    # Detekcia alebo nastavenie geometrických parametrov
547    square_side, amplitude = detect_geometry(data, args.square, args.amplitude)
548    print(f"--> Parametre geometrie: Štvorec S = {square_side:.1f} m, Osmička A = {amplitude:.1f} m")
549
550    # 1. Vygenerovanie samostatných detailných grafov pre dokumentáciu:
551    has_states = 'state' in data and any(s in ('SQUARE', 'FIGURE8') for s in data['state'])
552    data_sq = filter_by_states(data, {'TO_START', 'SQUARE'}) if has_states else data
553    data_f8 = filter_by_states(data, {'FIGURE8'}) if has_states else data
554
555    plot_single_square(data_sq, square_side=square_side, output_file="plot_1_square.png")
556    plot_single_figure8(data_f8, amplitude=amplitude, output_file="plot_2_figure8.png")
557    plot_single_yaw_error(data, output_file="plot_3_yaw_error.png")
558
559    # 2. Vygenerovanie hlavného súhrnného 6-panelového grafu:
560    plot_comprehensive(data, square_side=square_side, amplitude=amplitude, output_file=args.output, show=not args.no_show)
561

564    main()

L47–54

Install

Executables go to lib/lrs_psenak_valko, which is where ros2 run looks. Maps, config and missions go to share/. ament_package() generates the package metadata.

L9–26

Dependencies and build type

buildtool_depend: built with ament_cmake. Each depend is needed to build and to run (mirrors CMakeLists). build_type tells colcon how to build it.

29Examiner questions (flashcards)

70 questions. Try to answer aloud before opening. Search filters these too.

System

SystemWhat does each of the three simulation terminals run, and what are their arguments?
  • run_gazebo.sh: gz sim -r -v 4 fei_lrs_gazebo.world. -r = start running (not paused), -v 4 = verbose log; the world is the hangar with racks and the drone.
  • run_sitl.sh: sim_vehicle.py -v ArduCopter -f gazebo-iris --model JSON --add-param-file=gazebo-iris.parm --console -l <lat,lon,alt,heading>. ArduCopter firmware; --model JSON = talk to Gazebo's ArduPilot plugin; the param file sets the frame (otherwise it will not arm); --console = MAVProxy console window; -l = home location (FEI hangar).
  • run_mavros.sh: mavros_node with fcu_url:=udp://0.0.0.0:14550@: listen on UDP 14550, where MAVProxy forwards MAVLink.
SystemWho actually controls the motors?

ArduPilot. Our node only sends position/yaw targets and commands (GUIDED mode, arm, takeoff, land). ArduPilot's EKF estimates the state and its controllers compute motor outputs.

SystemWhy ROS 2 + MAVROS instead of talking MAVLink directly?

MAVROS handles the MAVLink connection, heartbeats, command acknowledgements and the NED↔ENU conversion, and exposes everything as ordinary ROS topics/services. Our node is then a normal ROS node that can talk to the ROS planner and be recorded with rosbag.

State machine

State machineList the states of your state machine.

IDLE, WAIT_FCU, SET_MODE, ARM, TAKEOFF, PLAN, WAIT_PLAN, WAIT_PREFETCH, ALIGN_PATH, NAVIGATE, TASK, LAND, WAIT_LANDED, WAIT_DISARMED, GROUND_WAIT, RETAKEOFF, DONE, ERROR. The assignment's IDLE → SET_MODE → ARM → TAKEOFF → NAVIGATE → TASK → … → LAND → DONE is the backbone; the others are waiting states and landtakeoff.

State machineHow do you check service responses?

Every call is async_send_request with a callback. The callback checks the response (mode_sent, success) and retries or fails. A request id ignores answers that belong to a previous state; a pending call older than 5 s fails the mission. For GUIDED and arming, the decision to move on is taken only from /mavros/state, the FCU's real state.

State machineWhat happens if something goes wrong in the air?

fail() logs the reason and enters ERROR, where the target becomes the current measured position, so the copter hovers in place (GUIDED position targets are latched). Triggers: state timeout, refused service, no answer, link lost, telemetry stale, unexpected disarm, mode changed away from GUIDED (pilot override: we do not fight it).

State machineWhy is all the logic in tick() and not in the callbacks?

One place makes decisions at a fixed rate with a consistent snapshot of the data (the mutex is held for the whole tick). Callbacks only store data. This avoids races and makes the behaviour deterministic and easy to log.

State machineHow are state transitions logged?

transition() logs Mission state: X through RCLCPP_INFO, which carries a timestamp. The CSV log also has the state in every 20 Hz row.

Threads

ThreadsExplain the callback groups and the executor.

Two MutuallyExclusive groups: subscriptions in state_group_, the 20 Hz timer in timer_group_, run by a MultiThreadedExecutor. The timer never waits behind a burst of messages. Because callbacks can then run in parallel, all shared members are protected by one mutex. Same pattern as mt_executor_demo.

ThreadsCould the mutex cause a deadlock?

No. There is only one mutex, and nothing waits for anything while holding it: service calls are asynchronous, so tick() sends and returns. The response callback takes the mutex later, in another thread.

ThreadsWhy steady_clock and not ROS time?

Timeouts and retries must use a clock that never jumps. The node does not use sim time; on a slow simulation the run script scales the timeouts by the measured real-time factor.

Takeoff & land

Takeoff & landWhy are no setpoints sent during takeoff?

In GUIDED, a position target makes ArduCopter abandon NAV_TAKEOFF and switch to position control, which does not lift a landed copter. The motors idle, and after DISARM_DELAY (10 s) it disarms. That was our first bug. Setpoints are only streamed in flight states.

Takeoff & landHow is 'airborne' confirmed?

Local z ≥ max(start + 0.5 m, target − 0.15 m) while armed. If it does not climb within takeoff_climb_timeout_sec, NAV_TAKEOFF is re-sent; if it disarms on the ground, GUIDED → arm → takeoff is retried (max 3).

Takeoff & landDescribe landtakeoff.

LAND command → wait for touchdown (z ≤ 0.15 m, speed ≤ 0.15 m/s) → wait for LAND mode to disarm (fallback disarm after 5 s, retried) → stay on the ground 3 s → GUIDED again (the copter is in LAND) → arm → takeoff to the waypoint's z → next waypoint.

Takeoff & landWhy did the disarm get refused, and how did you fix it?

ArduCopter accepts a disarm only when its own land detector says "landed", about a second after our touchdown condition. We now wait for LAND mode to disarm by itself and only then send our own disarm, retrying instead of failing.

Takeoff & landWhat does 'ArduPilot may disarm after landing' mean for you?

It always does in LAND mode. So after a landtakeoff we must re-enter GUIDED and arm again; the state machine goes GROUND_WAIT → SET_MODE → ARM → RETAKEOFF.

Navigation

NavigationHow is the route between two mission waypoints obtained?

The executor publishes plan_request (PoseArray: start, goal). The planner runs A* and answers on planned_path + planning_status. The first leg starts at the current pose; later legs are prefetched from waypoint to waypoint while flying.

NavigationWhat is prefetching and why?

While flying to waypoint i, the route i → i+1 is already requested. When the drone arrives, the route is ready (WAIT_PREFETCH is usually instant), so there is no hovering while A* runs.

NavigationWhat is carrot following and why did you add it?

The setpoint is a point that moves along the planned segment at cruise speed, never more than 1 m ahead of the drone. Before, only the segment end was sent; ArduCopter then used different horizontal and vertical speed limits and left the line by up to 0.56 m. The A* route is only collision-free on the line, which matters most when climbing over a rack.

NavigationDoes the drone stop at every intermediate point?

No. Route points are taken within 0.45 m without stopping. It only stops to turn at corners sharper than 20° (ALIGN_PATH), and at hard waypoints. A* simplification leaves few points.

NavigationWhat happens if the planner moves the goal?

If the goal is inside the inflated space, A* snaps it to the nearest free voxel (≤ 0.75 m). The executor compares the route end with the mission goal: further than the waypoint tolerance (0.15 hard / 0.55 soft) = fail, because we would not really reach the waypoint.

Precision

PrecisionHow do you define 'arrived' for hard and soft waypoints? Justify the values.

Hard: within 0.15 m and speed ≤ 0.2 m/s continuously for 1 s, from at least 2 distinct velocity messages. Soft: within 0.55 m, no stop. 0.15 m is in the suggested 10–20 cm range and is reached reliably in SITL (2.9–9.7 cm measured over all runs). 0.55 m lets the drone pass smoothly while already turning to the next leg. Tolerances never act as collision margins; the safety radius does.

PrecisionWhy count distinct velocity messages?

The loop runs at 20 Hz but velocity may arrive at ~10 Hz or less. Without the sequence counter, one old slow message seen 20 times could fake "1 s stable".

Yaw & frames

Yaw & framesWhat is your yaw convention?

Absolute heading in the map frame, degrees, counter-clockwise positive, 0° = map +x axis. yaw90 faces map +y; yaw-45 is 45° clockwise from +x. Not relative to the current heading.

Yaw & framesENU vs NED: where do you convert?

Nowhere. MAVROS converts NED↔ENU. We only convert between MAVROS's local ENU and the planner's map frame, which are rotated by 90° about z.

Yaw & framesHow do you know the map frame is rotated by 90°?

At start MAVROS reports local (−7.00, 12.99) for a drone that spawns at (12.99, 7.00) in the Gazebo world, which is the map frame (the rack boxes in the world match the map racks). x_map = y_local, y_map = −x_local, so the offset is 90°.

Yaw & framesHow does the drone face the direction of travel without spinning?

Target yaw = atan2(Δy, Δx) of the current segment, unwrapped near the previous yaw (unwrapNear), so it always takes the turn of at most 180°. That is why the log can show "−540°": it is the continuous (unwrapped) value, the same direction as 180°.

Yaw & framesHow is yaw sent to ArduPilot?

As the orientation of the position setpoint: quaternion (0, 0, sin ψ/2, cos ψ/2) with ψ = map yaw + 90°. Position + yaw targets are latched in GUIDED, so no velocity stream is needed and GUID_TIMEOUT does not apply.

Planner

PlannerHow is the map built?

620 k points → PCL VoxelGrid at 0.15 m → sparse hash set of occupied voxels → rack boxes filled solid (the cloud only has their surfaces) → every voxel inflated by a 0.45 m sphere → copied into a dense 3D array for A*.

PlannerWhy fill the racks?

The point cloud contains only the shelf surfaces; between the levels it looks free. Without filling, A* would plan through the shelves. Filling uses the real collision box sizes.

PlannerWhat heuristic do you use and why is it admissible?

The 3D octile distance res·[(√3 − √2)·d1 + (√2 − 1)·d2 + d3], d sorted ascending. It is the exact cost of the cheapest path in empty space on a 26-connected grid. Obstacles can only make paths longer, so it never overestimates. It is also consistent, so the first time the goal is popped the path is optimal and no node is re-expanded.

PlannerWhy not Manhattan or Euclidean distance?

Manhattan overestimates when diagonal moves are allowed (one diagonal step costs √2 but Manhattan counts 2): inadmissible, may give non-optimal paths. Euclidean is admissible but smaller than octile, so A* expands more voxels.

PlannerWhat is the corner-cutting check?

A diagonal move is allowed only if every voxel in the box it crosses is free; otherwise the path could slip between two blocked voxels that only touch at an edge/corner.

PlannerHow do you guarantee termination?

Each voxel is closed at most once, so the open list empties (NO_PATH) if the goal is unreachable; a time limit (5 s) gives TIMEOUT on huge maps.

PlannerWhat does path simplification do, and is it safe?

Greedy line of sight: from each point jump to the farthest later point visible in the inflated map (segment sampled every quarter voxel). Every final segment is checked again before publishing. It is safe because the checks use the inflated map.

PlannerWhat is A*'s complexity?

O(E log V) with a binary heap; V = voxels expanded (at most all free ones), E ≤ 26·V. Memory: g, parent and closed per voxel (~9 bytes).

Safety

SafetyHow did you choose the safety radius?

0.30 m. Drone radius is 0.33 m (body 0.35 m square, propellers at ±0.15 m), but routes run through voxel centres, so the measured closest approach to a rack was 0.37 m (4 cm from the props). 0.45 m gave 0.61 m but closes narrow passages and waypoints near walls. It is a parameter, so it can be raised at the defence.

SafetyHow do you prove there were no collisions?

plot_a1_2.py computes, for every logged pose, the distance to the nearest map point (walls, pillars, trusses, floor excluded) or rack surface with a k-d tree, and reports the minimum against the drone radius. All logged missions stayed above 0.33 m (minimum 0.37 m, over the rack). The final recording is in results/.

SafetyWhy does the measured clearance differ from the safety radius?

Voxel quantization. Inflation grows spheres around voxel centres, but the real obstacle surface can be up to half a voxel (0.075 m) closer than the centre of its voxel. Conversely, the route runs through free voxel centres, so it can also be farther. For a guaranteed clearance r, use r + half a voxel.

SafetyWhat is the weakest point of your solution?

Clearance over the racks: 0.37–0.38 m, only 4–5 cm more than the drone radius, from voxel quantization and, in test_over_racks, from the drone sinking ~0.15 m below its route on a descent. Options: a larger safety radius (0.5 m) at the cost of rejecting waypoints near walls, or a lower cruise speed on descents.

Running

RunningHow do you run an arbitrary mission file at the defence?

./run_a1_2.sh /path/to/mission.csv. The parser validates it first; the executor plans every leg with A*.

RunningWhat does the run script check before starting the executor?

Gazebo publishes world stats (and its real-time factor), SITL booted, MAVROS has the set_mode/arming/takeoff/land services, /mavros/state is connected, a local pose arrives, and the planner node exists. GPS/EKF readiness is left to the executor.

RunningWhy does arming take 20–40 s?

ArduPilot's pre-arm check needs the two simulated IMUs to agree for about 10 s of simulated time ("Gyros inconsistent" before that). At a real-time factor of 0.25–0.3 that is 30–40 s of wall time. The executor retries every 2 s and logs the reason.

RunningWhat is in the rosbag?

/mavros/state, local pose and velocity, setpoints, FCU texts, plan requests, planned paths, planning status and the markers: enough to replay and plot the mission.

RunningWhy do some plot lines have gaps?

During takeoff, landing and on the ground no setpoint is sent (ArduCopter flies those phases itself), so "commanded" values and the distance to target do not exist then. Measured values continue; the periods are labelled.

A1.1

A1.1How do you answer an occupancy query for an arbitrary 3D point?

Publish a PointStamped on /occupancy_query; the planner answers on /occupancy_result (true = blocked in the inflated map or outside the grid). Inside, the point is converted to a voxel index and looked up in the hash set (or the dense array).

A1.1What are the map's bounding box and voxel counts?

x −0.30…17.95, y −1.15…13.20, z 0…5.95 m; 620 311 points → 62 689 after the 0.15 m VoxelGrid; 73 069 occupied voxels with the filled racks; 234 353 after inflating by 0.30 m; dense A* grid 128 × 102 × 46.

A1.1Why is a voxel grid not 'stacked 2D layers'?

Occupancy and the search are in 3D: A* moves to all 26 neighbours, including diagonals up and down, and inflation is a 3D sphere. A route can climb diagonally over a rack, which no stack of 2D maps can represent.

A1.1Show that the safety radius changes the path.

Route (12, 4, 1) → (4.3, 6.8, 4.8) over the rack: 0.30 m → 3 points, 9.12 m; 0.75 m → 5 points, 9.55 m, climbing higher and earlier (docs/A1_1 figure). Inflated voxels grow from 234 353 to 416 757.

A1.1Give point counts before and after simplification.

Mission leg (8.65, 2.02, 1) → (4.84, 5.37, 2): 31 → 3 points, 5.94 → 5.48 m. Long diagonal (16.5, 1, 1) → (1, 9.1, 3): 104 → 3 points, 19.45 → 18.18 m. Every simplified route is re-checked for collisions.

A1.1Show the 'no path' case.

Goal inside the closed office (3, 1.5, 0.8): both endpoints are free, A* explores the whole reachable space (331 458 voxels in ~0.57 s) and returns NO_PATH with the closest point reached. A goal inside a rack gives GOAL_OCCUPIED, outside the map GOAL_OUT_OF_BOUNDS.

A1.1Which rack size did you use, and why does it differ from the assignment text?

6.50 × 1.05 × 3.76 m per rack, measured from the collision shapes in the current world file (uprights 3.76 m tall, footprint 6.5 × 1.05 m). The assignment mentions 6.5 × 3.76 × 5.59 m, which does not match the current world.

A1.1What is the planner's known weakness?

Holes in the roof scan along the ridge: a goal above the roof (9, 6, 6.35) was reachable through them. Missions never place goals there; a box in rack_boxes or a maximum altitude would close it.

A1.3

A1.3How is the origin defined, and why not a hard-coded coordinate?

After the fix has been usable (≥ 8 satellites, σ ≤ 2 m) for 3 s without interruption, the mean of those fixes is the origin and the local pose at that moment is the launch point. Everything else is in metres from it. The real drone's home differs every session.

A1.3How do you convert GPS to metres, and what is the error?

A local tangent plane: east = Δlon·(N + h)·cos φ0, north = Δlat·(M + h) with WGS-84 radii at the origin. Error ~d²/(2R): 0.25 mm at 50 m, ~0.003 mm on the mission. GPS is only used for the origin; flight uses ArduPilot's smooth local position.

A1.3Compass heading vs ENU yaw?

Compass: 0° = North, clockwise. ENU yaw: 0° = East, counter-clockwise. yaw_ENU = 90° − heading, wrapped to (−180, 180]. Verified at start (compass 0°, yaw 90°) and in flight (mean difference 0.14°).

A1.3Which altitude do you command and which do you check?

Command local z = launch z + 5 m (ArduPilot's local frame). Check both local z − launch z and rel_alt (above home): they differed by ≤ 1 cm. AMSL (~194 m) is only printed.

A1.3How does the nose stay on the POI?

Every tick (30 Hz) the bearing from the measured position, extrapolated 0.5 s by the velocity, to the POI; the yaw command slews towards it at ≤ 45°/s. Only yaw is controlled (fixed camera); the look-down angle (54–64°) is logged. Max error 3.3°.

A1.3Why a velocity feed-forward?

With position only, ArduPilot lags behind a moving setpoint by ~speed/gain (0.8 m measured). Sending the carrot's velocity too lets it track with ~0.1 m lag.

A1.3How is the figure-8 generated, and how did you choose ω?

A 30 Hz timer computes θ(t) and the point (A sin θ, A sin θ cos θ) around the POI, plus the analytic velocity. Peak speed A·ω·√2; for 1.5 m/s ω ≤ 0.35. Chosen 0.30 (1.27 m/s, lap 21 s) because 0.35 would need 64°/s of yaw at the loop ends, above ArduPilot's 60°/s default.

A1.3How is the 'lunge' into the figure-8 avoided?

First fly to the POI, stop, and turn to the start tangent (45°). Then θ̇ ramps from 0 to ω over 3 s (and back to 0 at the end), because θ = 0 is where the curve is fastest.

A1.3Tangent yaw: angle or rate?

Angle: yaw = atan2(cos 2θ, cos θ), unwrapped and evaluated slightly ahead. With a yaw rate small errors would accumulate; an angle corrects itself.

A1.3What is the difference between ABORTING and STOPPED?

ABORTING: our own decision (abort service, geofence, tracking error, timeout): switch to the abort mode (LAND). STOPPED: the pilot changed mode, the vehicle disarmed or MAVROS dropped out: send nothing and change nothing, never fight the pilot.

A1.3Why is A1.3 single-threaded when A1.2 is multi-threaded?

With one thread callbacks and the timer never run at the same time, so no mutex is needed and there are no races. A1.2 followed the mt_executor_demo pattern the assignment asks for, with two callback groups and a mutex.

A1.3How is the maths tested?

outdoor_math_test.cpp, built with plain g++: compass conversions, wrapping, GPS round trip, figure-8 size and speed, yaw continuity, ramp continuity, square corners, slew direction.

C++

C++What does std::bind(&MissionExecutorNode::onPose, this, std::placeholders::_1) do?

It creates a callable that calls the member function onPose on this object, passing the message as the first argument (_1).

C++What is a lambda capture like [this, request_id]?

The lambda copies request_id and the this pointer, so the response callback, which runs later, can access the node and knows which request it answers.

C++What is std::lock_guard?

An RAII lock: it locks the mutex in its constructor and unlocks in its destructor at the end of the scope, also on early return or exception.

C++Why enum class?

Scoped and strongly typed: State::ARM cannot be confused with an int or with another enum's value.

C++Why is the planner's plan() const?

It does not modify the planner; all search data are local. That documents and guarantees there are no side effects, and would allow concurrent searches.

31Glossary

Admissible heuristic
Never overestimates the remaining cost; A* is then optimal.
Consistent heuristic
h(a) ≤ cost(a, b) + h(b); no voxel is expanded twice.
Asynchronous service call
Send the request, continue; a callback handles the answer later.
Callback
A function the executor calls when something happens (message, timer, service answer).
Carrot following
Steering towards a moving point on the path a little ahead of the vehicle.
Clearance
Distance from the drone centre to the nearest obstacle point.
EKF
Extended Kalman Filter, ArduPilot's state estimator.
FCU
Flight Control Unit, the autopilot (here ArduCopter SITL).
GUIDED
ArduCopter mode that follows external targets.
Inflation
Growing obstacles by the safety radius.
k-d tree
Spatial index for fast nearest-neighbour queries (used for clearance).
Lazy deletion
Leaving outdated entries in the priority queue and skipping them when popped.
Lock-step
Simulator and firmware advance one step at a time together.
MAVLink
Message protocol between autopilot and ground/companion software.
MAVROS
ROS package bridging MAVLink and ROS.
Prefetch
Requesting the next leg's route while still flying the current one.
QoS
Quality of Service, delivery rules of a ROS topic.
RAII
Resource Acquisition Is Initialisation: resources (locks, files) are released by destructors, e.g. lock_guard.
RTF
Real-time factor of the simulation.
Setpoint
A target value sent to a controller.
SITL
Software In The Loop.
Snapping
Moving a blocked start/goal to the nearest free voxel.
STATUSTEXT
MAVLink text message from the autopilot (PreArm reasons etc.).
Transient local
QoS durability: late subscribers receive the last message.
Unwrap
Choosing the equivalent angle nearest to the previous one.
Voxel
A cube cell of a 3D grid.
